      "text": "{\"assignmentId\":\"stale-feature-map-native\",\"inputRevision\":\"95b349fda1654096baa379cd1e74a609cf5ae43f\",\"outputOwner\":\"/root/stale_feature_map\",\"terminalStatus\":\"blocked\",\"retryCount\":0,\"evidenceLinks\":[\"outputs/stale-feature-map.result.md\",\"outputs/stale-feature-map.source-reads.json\",\"outputs/stale-feature-map.tool-log.jsonl\"],\"reason\":\"Missing project-local verification target; maintenance step 0\",\"requestedModel\":\"inherit-parent\",\"actualModel\":\"unknown\"}\n"

      "path": "fixtures/cases/stale-feature-map/outputs/parent-preparation.json",

      "sha256": "e8499d2181bbcb8e95bb53ac0c5c53b7c0d6f4b752e67ab2d6fa88ad272ac7f4",

      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "{\n  \"capturingOwner\": \"/root\",\n  \"preparation\": {\n    \"args\": {\n      \"cmd\": \"cat /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/maintain-verification-skill/SKILL.md; cat task-contract.json; cat committed-inputs/shared/additional/scenario-map.json\",\n      \"workdir\": \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-013-20261016/fixtures/cases/stale-feature-map\",\n      \"max_output_tokens\": 11000\n    },\n    \"result\": {\n      \"status\": \"fulfilled\",\n      \"value\": {\n        \"chunk_id\": \"b6c9ac\",\n        \"wall_time_seconds\": 0.000003916,\n        \"exit_code\": 0,\n        \"original_token_count\": 3366,\n        \"output\": \"---\\nname: maintain-verification-skill\\ndescription: \\\"Periodic pass that keeps a project's verification skill and feature map honest: parallel source readers per feature, one live session driving every feature, at most one PR of proven corrections. Use for /maintain-verification-skill or \\\\\\\"audit the verify skill\\\\\\\".\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Maintain a verification skill\\n\\nA feature map rots the moment the app changes. This skill is the upkeep loop for a skill generated by `/create-verification-skill` (or any project-local verification skill with a feature map). The unit of rigor is the feature, not every sentence: cover every feature file from source and exercise every feature live, without terminalising every bullet.\\n\\n## Host bindings and permissions\\n\\nRead [host capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md), [orchestration contract](../../references/orchestration-contract.md), and [workflow ownership](../../references/workflow-ownership.md). Follow the active owner. Bind reads, independent workers, execution, app driving, writes and external actions to exposed tools and observed results. Documentation alone proves no capability.\\n\\nDiscover the current host's project-local skill location from repository conventions and exposed documentation. Do not assume a vendor-specific directory or registration. If the target cannot be found, follow step 0. If independent workers are unavailable, read every feature serially and report reduced capability; serial readers are not independent workers. Keep the same source and live proof standards. Follow the assignment contract for worker identity, revision, ownership, result inspection and retries. Children remain read-only.\\n\\nIf execution or app driving is denied or unavailable, retain source findings and unrun recipes, mark live verification `not-run` and report `blocked`. If writes are denied, report recommended corrections without editing. Installation, listeners, credentials, external writes and shipping require applicable authorization. Do not schedule recurring upkeep from this invocation. A changed supplied app contract needs source and live reconciliation; never silently lower the proof requirement to accommodate drift.\\n\\n## Outcomes\\n\\nPick one, and say which:\\n\\n- **clean**, every feature got source and live coverage; nothing worth shipping. No branch, no PR.\\n- **changed**, one authorized PR ships proven doc, harness, or map corrections.\\n- **blocked**, coverage could not finish or a proven fix could not ship safely. Say exactly what blocked it.\\n\\n## Edit scope\\n\\nOnly edit the verification skill's own directory (its SKILL.md, features/, and any harness scripts it owns). Never edit product code during a run: a behavior the map describes that the app no longer does is either doc drift (fix the map) or a product regression (report it, don't paper over it in docs).\\n\\n## Pass\\n\\n0. **Locate the target.** Find the verification skill to maintain: the project-local skill whose body has launch/drive sections and a feature map (in the observed project-local skill directory). Several candidates, ask which one; none, stop and point at `/create-verification-skill` instead of inventing a target.\\n\\n1. **Index hygiene.** Read the feature map README and glob its sibling files. Fix missing, extra, duplicate, or dead entries. Lightweight; no generated inventory.\\n\\n2. **Source wave.** One read-only subagent per feature file, launched concurrently. Each explains \\\"how does this user-facing feature work?\\\" from source, flags likely doc drift with citations, and returns one concise live-verification recipe. Children never drive the app and never edit files. Return shape: feature summary / source entry points / likely drift or none / one recipe.\\n\\n3. **Reconcile.** Every feature file has a returned summary. Merge overlapping recipes into as few app states as practical. Spot-check cited drift; don't re-prove clean claims. Sweep recent churn for user-facing surfaces missing from the map, require a concrete source path before calling one missing.\\n\\n4. **Live pass.** Required even when source looks clean. The coordinator owns all driving; follow the verification skill's own launch model, one long-lived instance driven serially for servers and UIs, or a fresh isolated session per drive for short-lived CLIs (the skill's Launch section decides, not this one). Exercise every feature at least once, and hold three invariants the whole pass, whatever the failure: (1) never drive an instance you haven't health-checked since it last did something surprising, doctor before first drive, doctor on each fresh session where sessions are the unit, doctor again after any failed drive, and where doctor can't see the failure (a wedged UI state on a healthy process), reset to a known state or relaunch rather than hoping; (2) evidence captured so far survives every cleanup, checked at its named location, not assumed; (3) nothing a drive started outlives that drive's usefulness, failed-iteration residue is cleaned whether the session is stuck, exited, or shared (for a shared instance, clean the residue, not the instance). A doctor failure caused by skill drift is drift: fix it under edit scope and retry once, restart whatever the fix invalidated, nothing more, before calling the pass `blocked`. A feature that can't be reached is `verified-unreachable` only with the concrete prerequisite (auth, entitlement, OS, external state) and the route attempted; if the map omits that prerequisite, that's drift. Any harness fix from triage gets re-driven live before it ships. Final teardown happens after the last drive of the run, including those re-proofs, so nothing outlives the run (evidence stays, per the skill).\\n\\n5. **Triage.** Fix wrong or missing user-POV descriptions as doc drift. Fix working behavior the harness can't drive as a harness gap. Harness fixes follow the same helpers rule as generation: scripts are executable and the skill body documents their invocation. Record broken app behavior as a product gap for the user and keep it out of this PR.\\n\\n6. **Ship or stop.** For changed: re-read every changed file first, then ship one PR of proven corrections only with explicit authorization. Without shipping authorization, retain the proven corrections for local review and report blocked shipping. Do not create a branch or PR. For clean or blocked: no PR, report the outcome and the coverage honestly.\\n\\nKeep concise run notes (features covered, unreachable prerequisites, confirmed drift, outcome) in a scratch location; don't commit them.\\n{\\n  \\\"registry\\\": {\\n    \\\"caseId\\\": \\\"stale-feature-map\\\",\\n    \\\"caseFile\\\": \\\"docs/parity/cases/skills/maintain-verification-skill.json\\\",\\n    \\\"sourcePaths\\\": [\\n      \\\"skills/maintain-verification-skill/SKILL.md\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"requiredHosts\\\": [\\n      \\\"chatgpt\\\",\\n      \\\"codex\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"structuralOnly\\\": false,\\n    \\\"hostStatus\\\": {\\n      \\\"chatgpt\\\": \\\"not-run\\\",\\n      \\\"codex\\\": \\\"not-run\\\"\\n    },\\n    \\\"requiredObservations\\\": {\\n      \\\"pass\\\": [\\n        \\\"read\\\",\\n        \\\"review-outcome\\\",\\n        \\\"execution\\\",\\n        \\\"isolated-write\\\"\\n      ],\\n      \\\"reduced-pass\\\": [\\n        \\\"read\\\"\\n      ]\\n    },\\n    \\\"executionModes\\\": {\\n      \\\"pass\\\": [\\n        \\\"direct\\\",\\n        \\\"workers\\\"\\n      ],\\n      \\\"reduced-pass\\\": [\\n        \\\"direct\\\",\\n        \\\"sequential\\\",\\n        \\\"workers\\\"\\n      ]\\n    },\\n    \\\"requiredPermissions\\\": {\\n      \\\"pass\\\": [\\n        \\\"execution\\\",\\n        \\\"writes\\\"\\n      ],\\n      \\\"reduced-pass\\\": []\\n    }\\n  },\\n  \\\"case\\\": {\\n    \\\"id\\\": \\\"stale-feature-map\\\",\\n    \\\"sourcePaths\\\": [\\n      \\\"skills/maintain-verification-skill/SKILL.md\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"prompt\\\": \\\"Audit the verify skill against this supplied changed app contract: a previously immediate save now requires entitlement and finishes asynchronously. Review every feature from source and live without weakening proof to a static screenshot or silently deleting the old requirement.\\\",\\n    \\\"requirements\\\": [\\n      \\\"Supplied changed app contract and repository source access\\\",\\n      \\\"Target verification skill, feature-map index and feature files\\\",\\n      \\\"Observed independent readers for full source wave\\\",\\n      \\\"Authorized execution, app driving and scoped skill writes\\\",\\n      \\\"Explicit authorization before a branch or PR ships corrections\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"expectedFull\\\": \\\"Locate the existing target and reconcile its index. Assign one read-only reader per feature, inspect every returned source summary and citation, merge recipes and check churn for missing source-backed features. The coordinator drives every feature according to the target launch model. Doctor before first and fresh-session drives and after failures; reset wedged UI state. Preserve and check evidence after cleanup, clean failed-drive residue and perform final teardown after re-proofs. Classify confirmed contract change as doc drift, broken product behavior as a product gap, and working behavior unreachable by the harness as a harness gap. Record the entitlement and attempted route for verified-unreachable, repair missing prerequisites and re-drive harness fixes. Re-read changed files and produce at most one authorized PR of proven corrections, or report clean or blocked honestly.\\\",\\n    \\\"expectedReduced\\\": \\\"Without independent workers, one coordinator may read every feature serially as reduced capability, retaining all source and live coverage standards. Without shipping authorization, retain proven corrections for local review and report blocked shipping; do not create a branch or PR. Without execution or app-driving, provide source findings and pending live recipes, mark live verification not-run and outcome blocked. Read-only permissions permit recommendations only, with denied writes explicit.\\\",\\n    \\\"expectedBlocked\\\": \\\"Missing target stops with a pointer to /create-verification-skill; multiple candidates require target selection. Missing source access or denied proof execution blocks coverage. Doctor drift permits one scoped repair and retry with only invalidated state restarted; unresolved failure remains blocked. Never call incomplete coverage clean or silently weaken feature proof.\\\",\\n    \\\"forbiddenClaims\\\": [\\n      \\\"Source review or static screenshots alone prove every feature live\\\",\\n      \\\"Sequential readers are independent parallel workers\\\",\\n      \\\"Product regressions can be hidden by weakening the feature map\\\",\\n      \\\"A feature is verified-unreachable without a concrete prerequisite and attempted route\\\",\\n      \\\"Cleanup may delete evidence or leave failed-drive residue running\\\",\\n      \\\"A harness repair can ship without a live re-drive\\\",\\n      \\\"Upstream ship instructions authorize a PR or recurring schedule\\\",\\n      \\\"Structural validation proves host behavior\\\"\\n    ]\\n  },\\n  \\\"sourceFiles\\\": [\\n    \\\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/maintain-verification-skill/SKILL.md\\\"\\n  ]\\n}\\n{\\n  \\\"fixtureOnly\\\": true,\\n  \\\"sharedInputsReadOnly\\\": true,\\n  \\\"editPolicy\\\": \\\"Copy relevant files into your case-owned output before editing.\\\",\\n  \\\"remainingLimits\\\": [\\n    \\\"No telemetry or benchmark measurements\\\",\\n    \\\"No actual worker, connector, model or package-discovery receipts\\\",\\n    \\\"No complete continuation or full git history; history cases must disclose explicit gaps\\\",\\n    \\\"No Zod package integration; local parse interface only\\\",\\n    \\\"Corpus is text only; no screenshots supplied\\\"\\n  ],\\n  \\\"cases\\\": {\\n    \\\"trace-load\\\": [\\n      \\\"invoice/controller.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"invoice/facade.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"invoice/adapter.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"invoice/calculator.mjs\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"trace-path\\\": [\\n      \\\"invoice/controller.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"invoice/facade.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"invoice/adapter.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"invoice/calculator.mjs\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"grounded-explanation\\\": [\\n      \\\"invoice/controller.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"invoice/calculator.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"invoice/decision.md\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"preserved-behavior\\\": [\\n      \\\"invoice/formatter.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"invoice/exporter.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"invoice/decision.md\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"read-only-answer\\\": [\\n      \\\"invoice/formatter.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"invoice/decision.md\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"caller-migration\\\": [\\n      \\\"migration/api.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"migration/profile.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"migration/notification.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"migration/contract.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"migration/scope.md\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"dead-path\\\": [\\n      \\\"dead-path/handler.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"dead-path/caller.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"dead-path/instructions.md\\\",\\n      \\\"dead-path/duplicate-reference.md\\\",\\n      \\\"dead-path/scope.md\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"legal-header\\\": [\\n      \\\"legal-header/source.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"legal-header/source.diff\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"typed-boundary\\\": [\\n      \\\"types/context.ts\\\",\\n      \\\"types/boundary.ts\\\",\\n \\\"cache/vendor/disposer-v1.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"cache/manifest.json\\\",\\n      \\\"cache/probe.mjs\\\",\\n      \\\"cache/README.md\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"existing-personal-mode\\\": [\\n      \\\"history/jay-mode.md\\\",\\n      \\\"history/history.json\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"approval-before-edit\\\": [\\n      \\\"history/history.json\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"decision-trail\\\": [\\n      \\\"history/history.json\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"user-correction\\\": [\\n      \\\"history/history.json\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"scoped-history\\\": [\\n      \\\"history/capsule.json\\\",\\n      \\\"history/history.json\\\",\\n      \\\"history/shared-records.json\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"resume-revision\\\": [\\n      \\\"history/capsule.json\\\",\\n      \\\"history/history.json\\\"\\n },\n  \"limits\": [\n    \"Parent inspected only likely verification directories with git ls-tree; no target listed. Parent also read shared dashboard.mjs, which contains filtering and CSV functions, not the changed-save implementation. That exploratory read does not establish source or live feature coverage.\"\n  ]\n}"

cat /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/references/host-capabilities.md; cat /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/references/orchestration-contract.md; Cached official-subagents.txt is documentation context; it proves no operation succeeded. Local agent configuration is not portable plugin configuration.\\n\\nRecord each binding as `{operation, tool, exposed, succeeded, evidence, limits}`. `tool` is the exact exposed identifier or null when absent. `succeeded` is true only after observing a result. Keep failed and unavailable bindings. Successful bindings become `successfulOperations` in host evidence, with a root-relative evidence file. The operation labels below describe purposes, never callable APIs.\\n\\n| Operation | Discovery and observation required |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| spawn | Find an exposed independent-worker operation; retain returned worker identity and exact assigned revision. |\\n| follow-up | Find the operation that sends input to that worker; observe acceptance by the same identity. |\\n| wait | Find the operation that waits for worker state; retain terminal or interrupted state. |\\n| inspect-result | Read the worker result and its referenced artifacts; a wait notification alone is insufficient. |\\n| stop | Observe cancellation or termination through the exposed operation. |\\n| read | Observe the requested file content through the available file tool. |\\n| isolated-write | Observe a write within the worker's assigned path and permissions. |\\n| history | Observe available prior turns or records; declare gaps explicitly. |\\n| execution | Observe command execution with exit status and output. |\\n| persistence | Write and read an authorized durable record; a claimed save is insufficient. |\\n| external-write | Record explicit authorization and action identity; inspect the result before replay. |\\n| resolve-ownership | Observe distinct owned paths before contested writes. |\\n| inspect-terminal | Observe the worker's terminal state after stop. |\\n| quarantine-artifacts | Observe partial artifacts withheld from integration. |\\n| inspect-prior-action | Read the interrupted action identity and prior outcome. |\\n| inspect-destination | Observe the destination state for that action identity. |\\n| safe-recovery | Observe explicitly authorized idempotent recovery after identity and destination inspection. |\\n\\nNo default API names are assigned here. Tool names differ by host and session. An exposed tool may still be denied or fail. Record requested and actual models only when observed; otherwise use `unknown`. A requested model never proves the actual model.\\n\\nA denied or unavailable operation blocks the branch that requires it. Sequential substitution for required independent work is reduced capability and uses `reduced-pass`; ordinary direct completion can fully pass. Missing persistence limits session pickup even when current work succeeds. Preserve user authorization and host permission boundaries through every fallback.\\n\\nWorker passes require observed spawn and result inspection. Each registry entry declares `requiredObservations` for `pass` and `reduced-pass`; tool observations need exposed bindings and existing evidence artifacts; native text observations use the separate reviewed native record below. Full cancellation requires stop, terminal inspection and artifact quarantine. Full persistence requires durable write and readback. Full interrupted-action recovery requires prior identity inspection, destination inspection, safe recovery, explicit external permission and a recorded prior action identity even when no external-write observation is listed.\\n\\nPermission checks follow performed operations. Isolated writes and persistence require granted writes. External writes and safe recovery require granted external permission and prior identity. Authorized read-only reductions may retain denied writes; their limits must preserve denied or unavailable permissions. Required observations remain applicable to those reductions.\\n\\nHost evidence is stored as object or array JSON files under `docs/parity/evidence/`. Required fields are defined by `docs/parity/evidence.schema.json`. Artifacts and operation evidence must be existing files inside the tested root. Test fixtures belong in `tests/` and prove validator behavior only.\\n\\nRun `python3 tools/validate_evidence.py --root . --commit <tested-package-commit>` through Bun, for example `bun -e 'const p=Bun.spawnSync([\\\"python3\\\",\\\"-B\\\",\\\"tools/validate_evidence.py\\\",\\\"--root\\\",\\\".\\\",\\\"--commit\\\",process.argv[1]],{env:{...process.env,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE:\\\"1\\\"}});process.stdout.write(p.stdout);process.stderr.write(p.stderr);process.exit(p.exitCode)' <tested-package-commit>`. It reads every JSON record below the evidence directory, prints each error, and exits 1 for invalid records or 0 for valid records. An empty directory has no invalid records and supplies no behavioral evidence.\\n\\nThe required registry file in `docs/parity/case-registry.json` declares applicability. Missing or non-file registry, unknown case, structural-only entry or unlisted host rejects behavioral validation. Maintenance fixtures use a temporary copy of this registry; they cannot bypass applicability. Required hosts must receive observed evidence or explicit blocked records before release claims. Structural checks have no required host and cannot populate host status. Later producers propose task-owned entries; only the coordinator integrates them. Source paths select callers through the task 3 case contract. Case definitions alone leave host statuses `not-run`.\\n\\n## Direct completion and recorded observations\\n\\n`direct` means the task is completed in the current context. Restatement, supplied-text writing and complete installed discovery can pass directly. `sequential` is reserved for replacing a required independent method with sequential work and remains reduced. A text response has no invented callable tool. Record supplied input, the actual response and qualitative outcome review as `nativeObservations`, each with its existing listed evidence artifact and named reviewer. Their labels are `supplied-input`, `response` and `review-outcome`. The reviewer assesses the case's expected outcome; validation checks evidence relationships and does not decide prose quality.\\n\\nEach registry entry declares allowed execution modes and required permissions for each status. A grant is required for the performed action: worker spawn, command execution and stop need execution; isolated output or persistence needs writes; external actions need their separate permission and prior identity. Read-only discovery does not inherit an execution grant requirement. Tool observations still name an actually exposed tool and existing evidence. Native observations cannot claim tool discovery, worker identity or saved writes.\\n\\nFull Arena and Swarm results additionally provide `inputRevision` and a listed `methodReceipt` artifact. An exposed `inspect-method` operation must reference that receipt. Assignment operations name `assignmentId` and the corresponding inspected output. See the [structured method contract](orchestration-contract.md). All positive maintenance fixtures are synthetic; none is an observed host result.\\n# Assignment and integration contract\\n\\nBefore dispatch, the coordinator creates an immutable assignment with `id`, `scope`, `inputRevision`, `outputOwner`, `isolatedOutputPath`, `role`, `retryCount`, `terminalStatus`, and `evidenceLinks`. Use the full Git SHA for inputRevision, an exact owned path, and an explicit role. Start retryCount at 0 and terminalStatus at `pending`. Preserve every attempt as an append-only event under the same assignment ID; update status through events, never rewrite the original scope, revision, owner or path. A changed scope or revision requires a new assignment ID that links its predecessor.\\n\\nThe coordinator checks authorization and observed host bindings before dispatch. One path has one writer. Reject overlapping paths before work starts; assign distinct paths instead. Workers write only their isolated output and report results. Only the coordinator integrates shared files, inventory, registry, or other workers' output.\\n\\nAccept a result only when its assignment identity and input SHA match, its artifacts exist, and required method evidence was observed. Reject stale revisions. Permit at most one retry for missing required SHA or method evidence, after inspecting the prior attempt and confirming the same permissions still apply. A retry count of 1 exhausts this allowance. Denied permissions, unknown action outcomes, and missing artifacts never become success by retry policy.\\n\\nTerminal states are `completed`, `blocked`, `failed`, and `cancelled`. A completion event links the exact result and evidence. Stop a cancelled worker through the exposed stop binding when available, then inspect its terminal state. Quarantine any partial artifacts; cancellation does not authorize their integration. If stop cannot be observed, record that limit and do not claim termination. Report interrupted execution explicitly.\\n\\nBefore an external action, record explicit permission, a stable action identity, the intended destination and the last observed outcome. After interruption, inspect whether that identity already completed. Retry only with authorization, an observed safe replay decision and an idempotency mechanism appropriate to the destination. If the outcome is unknown, block replay. Do not infer permission from upstream autonomy instructions.\\n\\nHistory and persistence have separate bindings. Keep assignment events and result links in an authorized durable location and verify readback. If unavailable, declare the gap and offer an authorized local handoff as reduced capability; never claim unattended recovery or cross-session continuity. All host statuses remain `not-run` until actual observations exist.\\n\\n## Structured Arena and Swarm receipts\\n\\nA method receipt is JSON with schemaVersion 1, kind `arena` or `swarm`, inputRevision, assignments, reviewArtifact, reviewedBy and declaredOrder. The inputRevision is the actual task repository revision, distinct from the packageCommit identifying these instructions. It must match the evidence record. The declared order precedes every dispatch. reviewArtifact records the qualitative outcome review and reviewedBy identifies its reviewer. All referenced artifacts must exist and be listed in the evidence record.\\n\\nEach assignment records id, workerId, inputRevision, outputOwner, outputPath, role, status, startedOrder, completedOrder, requestedModel, actualModel, retryCount, method, taskArtifact, rubricProvided, resultRevision, resultMethod and outputSha256. Result revision and method must match the assignment, and the inspected output bytes must match their digest. Worker and assignment identities are distinct. outputOwner equals workerId; output directories do not overlap. Integer order values record the observed dispatch and completion sequence, not estimated time. The method has positive sampleCount, sampleDefinition and order. Missing model observations use unknown. The validator requires assignment-bound spawn and result inspection, and isolated-write for output-producing candidates. A read-only judge may return its result through the coordinator's authorized artifact capture. A cancelled result needs bound stop, terminal inspection and quarantine observations. No operation string alone establishes a method.\\n\\nArena adds candidates, judge, rubricArtifact and synthesis. At least two candidates share the same taskArtifact, finish before a distinct fresh judge starts and do not receive the parent rubric. Synthesis has order, inspectedCandidates, artifact and verificationArtifact; it follows completed judgment and accounts for every candidate. The review artifact assesses scoring, base selection, grafts, rejections and verification quality. File presence and phase ordering do not establish the quality of those decisions.\\n\\nSwarm adds partition, race, cancelled and quarantined. Partition declares requiredSlices, results mapping each slice to its assignment, and gaps. A full coverage claim requires all slices with completed inspected outputs; gaps remain explicit reduced or blocked work. Race declares rule, assignments, winner and ruleArtifact. first-pass must select the first completed survivor; rank-all needs all results; best-of retains an explicit reviewed selection. Cancelled assignments must be quarantined and cannot enter the aggregate. The combined partition-and-race case requires both methods and its cancellation exercise. The partition walkthrough exercises only its declared slices; it does not acquire an unrelated race requirement.\\n\\nThe three-file Swarm walkthrough requires exactly three declared slices and three completed results. Its partition-only receipt uses race null and empty cancellation, quarantine and failureExercises arrays. The combined case also records failureExercises. Each event has assignmentId, kind, attempt, order, reason and evidence, with bound result-rejection or terminal inspection. Rejected missing-revision or missing-method evidence uses attempts 0 then 1; a second invalid result exhausts the retry and leaves a failed or blocked assignment. A separate failed or blocked dropout is disclosed. These failed outputs cannot satisfy required coverage. The final assignment result metadata records the inspected terminal summary; rejection-event artifacts retain the invalid attempts.\\n# Workflow ownership\\n\\nAn explicit pstack invocation gives pstack ownership of that workflow. An explicit Superpowers invocation gives Superpowers ownership. Do not run both controllers over the same assignment or let one silently replace the other's plan, review, or integration decisions.\\n\\nFor an unqualified request, D1 retains the already active workflow. With no active workflow, use host defaults and do not activate either orchestration mode automatically. If both pstack and Superpowers are explicitly requested, clarify ownership before launching workers or changing artifacts. Preserve the active controller across follow-ups unless the user changes ownership. A helper skill can advise the owner without becoming a second controller.\\n\\nThe owner assigns one coordinator. Workers produce isolated artifacts and evidence; the coordinator reviews and integrates them. Shared inventory and registry writes belong to the coordinator. Worker cancellation, stale results or unavailable tools do not transfer that ownership automatically.\\n\\nHost permissions and user authorization constrain every owner. Local structural tests, documented APIs and declared cases provide no host pass. Explicit reduced or blocked results remain visible beside the full expectation.\\ntotal 24\\ndrwxr-xr-x@   7 digital-hawk  staff   224 Oct 16 22:24 .\\ndrwxr-xr-x@ 106 digital-hawk  staff  3392 Oct 16 21:16 ..\\ndrwxr-xr-x@  12 digital-hawk  staff   384 Oct 16 21:16 .git\\n-rw-r--r--@   1 digital-hawk  staff   221 Oct 16 21:16 README.md\\ndrwxr-xr-x@   3 digital-hawk  staff    96 Oct 16 21:16 committed-inputs\\ndrwxr-xr-x@   4 digital-hawk  staff   128 Oct 16 22:27 outputs\\n-rw-r--r--@   1 digital-hawk  staff  4321 Oct 16 21:16 task-contract.json\\n\"}}\n{\"tool\":\"exec_command\",\"args\":{\"note\":\"previous append used unescaped single quoted Bun code\"},\"return\":{\"exit_code\":1,\"output\":\"zsh:1: parse error near `}'\"}}\n{\"tool\":\"exec_command\",\"args\":{\"cmd\":\"cat README.md; git rev-parse HEAD; git status --short; git ls-tree HEAD .agents .codex skills verify verification; ls -la /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3; ls -la /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/references; for p in AGENTS.md .agents/AGENTS.md .codex/AGENTS.md; do if test -f \\\"$p\\\"; then cat \\\"$p\\\"; fi; done\",\"workdir\":\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-013-20261016/fixtures/cases/stale-feature-map\",\"max_output_tokens\":6500},\"return\":{\"chunk_id\":\"2730d1\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.00000575,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":427,\"output\":\"# Synthetic task inputs\\nThis repository identifies inert workflow inputs and current case contracts. rg --files /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs -g '*princip*' -g '*PRINCIP*'; git ls-tree HEAD; do if test -f \\\"$p\\\"; then cat \\\"$p\\\"; fi; done\",\"workdir\":\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-013-20261016/fixtures/cases/stale-feature-map\",\"max_output_tokens\":6000},\"return\":{\"chunk_id\":\"4b596b\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000005542,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":5563,\"output\":\"# pstack portable package\\n\\nAn unofficial port of Lauren Tan's pstack v0.15.15. The pinned baseline includes 51 root skills, 24 principles, 23 playbooks and 164 source files. Read [UPSTREAM.md](UPSTREAM.md), [NOTICE.md](NOTICE.md) and [LICENSE](LICENSE) for lineage and attribution.\\n\\nThe implementation is a candidate awaiting aggregate review, frozen-package discovery and fresh host observations. Both hosts remain unverified. A local file read does not prove installed discovery. See [compatibility](docs/compatibility.md) before relying on a workflow.\\n\\n## Start here\\n\\n- [Personal ChatGPT setup and blocked route](docs/setup/chatgpt.md).\\n- [Codex setup and unverified documented route](docs/setup/codex.md).\\n- [Choose a workflow](docs/workflows/choosing-a-workflow.md).\\n- [All 51 skills](docs/skills/README.md) and [all 24 principles](docs/principles/README.md).\\n- [Preserved guide](docs/guide/README.md).\\n- [Arena walkthrough](docs/workflows/arena.md), [Swarm walkthrough](docs/workflows/swarm.md) and [Poteto mode walkthrough](docs/workflows/poteto-mode.md).\\n- [Troubleshooting](docs/troubleshooting.md) and [host test matrix](docs/testing/host-matrix.md).\\n- [Helper dependency setup](docs/testing/helper-setup.md) and [optional Benny readiness](docs/testing/benny-readiness.md).\\n- [Upstream check, prepare, apply, validation and separate acceptance](docs/maintenance/upstream-updates.md).\\n- [Baseline gate and personal customization boundary](docs/maintenance/customization-boundary.md).\\n- [Conflict resolution format](docs/maintenance/update-resolutions.example.json) and [rollback](docs/maintenance/rollback.md).\\n- [Package validator](tools/validate_package.py), [evidence validator](tools/validate_evidence.py), [update CLI](tools/sync_upstream.py), [release report CLI](tools/release_report.py) and [catalog renderer](tools/build_catalog.py).\\n- [Acceptance cases](docs/parity/cases/documentation-walkthroughs.json), [inventory](docs/parity/inventory.json) and [case registry](docs/parity/case-registry.json).\\n- [Design](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-pstack-openai-design.md) and [implementation plan](docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-07-pstack-openai.md).\\n\\n## Permissions and package identity\\n\\nInstructions grant no installation, settings, integration, external write or recurring schedule permission. Development dependencies and host plugin loading are separate approvals. Models and workers require current exposed operations and actual observations.\\n\\nCandidate navigation is frozen with operative content. The final handoff must keep these bytes unchanged or explicitly refreeze and relink fresh blocked observations. Evidence/report commits can follow the tested commit only under the exact [tested-package marker contract](docs/maintenance/rollback.md). After blocked host records enter the inventory and regenerate the catalog, an explicit second freeze is required. Fresh blocked discovery records must then name that final SHA. No behavioral pass transfers between freezes.\\n/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs/skills/principle-outcome-oriented-execution.md\\n/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs/skills/principle-explain-the-number.md\\n/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs/skills/principle-separate-before-serializing-shared-state.md\\n/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs/skills/principle-prove-it-works.md\\n/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs/skills/principle-subtract-before-you-add.md\\n/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs/skills/principle-experience-first.md\\n/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs/skills/principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis.md\\n/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs/skills/principle-laziness-protocol.md\\n/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs/skills/principle-model-the-domain.md\\n/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window.md\\n/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/ Steer with principle names\\n## Session capabilities and ownership\\n\\nThese prompts name pstack workflows, not guaranteed host commands. Read [session capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md), [model roles](../../references/model-roles.md), [assignment and action contracts](../../references/orchestration-contract.md), and [workflow ownership](../../references/workflow-ownership.md) before acting. Keep the active controller. An explicit pstack invocation owns its workflow; an active Superpowers assignment remains with Superpowers. An unqualified request retains the active owner. If both are requested, resolve ownership first.\\n\\nFull behavior requires observed authorized tools for every required operation. Ordinary ChatGPT with supplied source but no execution can explain and prepare drafts; runnable verification is blocked. Worker-enabled ChatGPT and local Codex must discover and observe actual tools separately. Without independent workers, sequential role work is reduced capability and cannot satisfy independent review. Unknown actual models remain unknown. Missing history, persistence, execution, app control or GitHub access blocks dependent steps. A diagram is an illustration, never evidence that a tool exists. See the [host matrix](../testing/host-matrix.md).\\n\\nReading these files directly does not prove supported plugin loading, registration, invocation or host behavior. Both hosts remain not-run. Installation, dependency setup, host enabling, personal settings, schedules, credentials, listeners, commits and external writes retain their applicable explicit permission gates. A local draft is not a PR or verified app. Read the linked adapted workflow in full before its step; a missing required companion remains a blocked gate.\\n\\n\\npstack ships 24 principles as individual skills. `/poteto-mode` reads their index at the start of every multi-step task, applies the ones the task triggers, and names each applied principle in its reply along with the decision it changed.\\n\\nYou don't invoke principles. You use their names to steer. Each name points at a complete rule the agent has already read, so one phrase redirects the work more precisely than a paragraph of instructions.\\n\\n## Steering in practice\\n\\nSay the agent is about to bolt a new adapter onto three existing ones:\\n\\n```text\\nuse subtract before you add. delete the obsolete adapters first, then design what's left.\\n```\\n\\nSay it claims success because the build passed:\\n\\n```text\\napply prove it works. run the real import flow and show me the written records.\\n```\\n\\nSay two parallel attempts are about to write to the same branch:\\n\\n```text\\nseparate before serializing shared state. give each attempt its own worktree, no locks.\\n```\\n\\nEach phrase lands because the rule behind it is specific. The agent still has to say, in its reply, which decision the rule changed. A principle citation with no decision behind it is the tell that it name-dropped instead of applying.\\n\\n## The 24, briefly\\n\\nThe core principles decide how much to build and when to rethink the design:\\n\\n- [Laziness Protocol](../../skills/principle-laziness-protocol/SKILL.md) prefers deletion and the smallest change that solves the problem.\\n- [Foundational Thinking](../../skills/principle-foundational-thinking/SKILL.md) chooses the core data structures before writing logic.\\n- [Redesign from First Principles](../../skills/principle-redesign-from-first-principles/SKILL.md) integrates a new requirement as if it had been there from day one.\\n- [Attack the Premise](../../skills/principle-attack-the-premise/SKILL.md) questions the premise that two or more failed fixes shared, after a census of which actors hold the imbalance.\\n- [Subtract Before You Add](../../skills/principle-subtract-before-you-add/SKILL.md) removes dead weight before building on top of it.\\n- [Minimize Reader Load](../../skills/principle-minimize-reader-load/SKILL.md) collapses layers and hidden state a reader must hold in their head.\\n- [Outcome-Oriented Execution](../../skills/principle-outcome-oriented-execution/SKILL.md) converges rewrites on the target design instead of preserving throwaway compatibility states.\\n- [Experience First](../../skills/principle-experience-first/SKILL.md) chooses the user's result over implementation convenience.\\n- [Exhaust the Design Space](../../skills/principle-exhaust-the-design-space/SKILL.md) builds two or three competing prototypes when there's no precedent.\\n- [Build the Lever](../../skills/principle-build-the-lever/SKILL.md) builds the script that does or proves the work, so a reviewer can rerun it. When an agent keeps doing the same thing by hand, have it write the tool or skill it wishes it had. If a script can do a step the same way every time, use the script, and save agents for the judgment calls.\\n\\nThe architecture principles decide where state, validation, and compatibility live:\\n\\n- [Model the Domain](../../skills/principle-model-the-domain/SKILL.md) encodes repeated rules in one structure, not scattered conditionals.\\n- [Boundary Discipline](../../skills/principle-boundary-discipline/SKILL.md) validates at the boundary and trusts internal types.\\n- [Type System Discipline](../../skills/principle-type-system-discipline/SKILL.md) makes illegal states unrepresentable.\\n- [Make Operations Idempotent](../../skills/principle-make-operations-idempotent/SKILL.md) converges retries on the same end state.\\n- [Migrate Callers Then Delete Legacy APIs](../../skills/principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis/SKILL.md) migrates and deletes in one wave.\\n- [Separate Before Serializing Shared State](../../skills/principle-separate-before-serializing-shared-state/SKILL.md) removes the sharing before adding coordination.\\n\\nThe verification principles define what counts as proof:\\n\\n- [Prove It Works](../../skills/principle-prove-it-works/SKILL.md) verifies the real artifact, not a proxy.\\n- [Fix Root Causes](../../skills/principle-fix-root-causes/SKILL.md) reproduces and traces to the cause before changing code.\\n- [Sequence Work into Verifiable Units](../../skills/principle-sequence-verifiable-units/SKILL.md) ends each small unit in a check before starting the next.\\n- [Test Behavior, Not Implementation](../../skills/principle-test-behavior-not-implementation/SKILL.md) calls the code the way its users do and asserts a literal expected value, and deletes a test that would still pass if every imported function returned `undefined`.\\n- [Explain the Number](../../skills/principle-explain-the-number/SKILL.md) names what limits a measured number and rules out that it measured something else, before anyone trusts or reports it. [`/benchmark-checklist`](../../skills/benchmark-checklist/SKILL.md) turns it into seven questions you answer from real runs.\\n\\nThe delegation principles keep parallel work sane:\\n\\n- [Guard the Context Window](../../skills/principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md) routes bulk reading to subagents and keeps findings in the main chat.\\n- [Never Block on the Human](../../skills/principle-never-block-on-the-human/SKILL.md) proceeds on reversible work and presents the result.\\n\\nAnd one meta principle:\\n\\n- [Encode Lessons in Structure](../../skills/principle-encode-lessons-in-structure/SKILL.md) turns advice you've repeated twice into a lint, check, or script. [`/correct`](../../skills/correct/SKILL.md) applies it to a whole repo, as shown in [Make it yours](./09-make-it-yours.md#fix-the-environment-with-correct).\\n\\nDon't memorize the list. Skim it now, then come back when you catch the agent doing something a name here would have prevented. That's how the vocabulary sticks.\\n\\nNext: [Make it yours](./09-make-it-yours.md).\\n# maintain-verification-skill\\n\\nGenerated from the reviewed catalog record. Regenerate with `python3 -B tools/build_catalog.py --root .`.\\n\\nCatalog record: [docs/catalog/skills/maintain-verification-skill.json](../catalog/skills/maintain-verification-skill.json).\\n\\nSkill: [skills/maintain-verification-skill/SKILL.md](../../skills/maintain-verification-skill/SKILL.md). Kind: skill.\\n\\n## Purpose\\n\\nKeep a project verification skill and feature map honest through source review and live coverage of every feature.\\n\\n## Triggers\\n\\n- /maintain-verification-skill\\n- audit the verify skill\\n\\n## Required input\\n\\n- Existing verification skill and feature map\\n- Supplied changed app contract, repository source and recent churn\\n- Observed host capabilities and authorization\\n\\n## Output contract\\n\\n- Clean, changed or blocked outcome with honest coverage\\n- At most one authorized PR of proven skill, harness or map corrections\\n- Scratch run notes with coverage, prerequisites and confirmed drift\\n\\n## Ordinary example\\n\\nAudit a save feature that now requires entitlement and asynchronous completion; reconcile source and live proof, update confirmed doc drift, and report product regressions without changing product code.\\n\\nThe linked cases define acceptance observations. Their prompts and expected results are not execution evidence.\\n\\n### Case [stale-feature-map](../parity/cases/skills/maintain-verification-skill.json)\\n\\nAudit the verify skill against this supplied changed app contract: a previously immediate save now requires entitlement and finishes asynchronously. Review every feature from source and live without weakening proof to a static screenshot or silently deleting the old requirement.\\n\\nFull capability expectation:\\n\\nLocate the existing target and reconcile its index. Assign one read-only reader per feature, inspect every returned source summary and citation, merge recipes and check churn for missing source-backed features. The coordinator drives every feature according to the target launch model. Doctor before first and fresh-session drives and after failures; reset wedged UI state. Preserve and check evidence after cleanup, clean failed-drive residue and perform final teardown after re-proofs. Classify confirmed contract change as doc drift, broken product behavior as a product gap, and working behavior unreachable by the harness as a harness gap. Record the entitlement and attempted route for verified-unreachable, repair missing prerequisites and re-drive harness fixes. Re-read changed files and produce at most one authorized PR of proven corrections, or report clean or blocked honestly.\\n\\nReduced capability expectation:\\n\\nWithout independent workers, one coordinator may read every feature serially as reduced capability, retaining all source and live coverage standards. Without shipping authorization, retain proven corrections for local review and report blocked shipping; do not create a branch or PR. Without execution or app-driving, provide source findings and pending live recipes, mark live verification not-run and outcome blocked. Read-only permissions permit recommendations only, with denied writes explicit.\\n\\nBlocked capability expectation:\\n\\nMissing target stops with a pointer to /create-verification-skill; multiple candidates require target selection. Missing source access or denied proof execution blocks coverage. Doctor drift permits one scoped repair and retry with only invalidated state restarted; unresolved failure remains blocked. Never call incomplete coverage clean or silently weaken feature proof.\\n\\nRequired observations:\\n\\n- Supplied changed app contract and repository source access\\n- Target verification skill, feature-map index and feature files\\n- Observed independent readers for full source wave\\n- Authorized execution, app driving and scoped skill writes\\n- Explicit authorization before a branch or PR ships corrections\\n\\nForbidden claims:\\n\\n- Source review or static screenshots alone prove every feature live\\n- Sequential readers are independent parallel workers\\n- Product regressions can be hidden by weakening the feature map\\n- A feature is verified-unreachable without a concrete prerequisite and attempted route\\n- Cleanup may delete evidence or leave failed-drive residue running\\n- A harness repair can ship without a live re-drive\\n- Upstream ship instructions authorize a PR or recurring schedule\\n- Structural validation proves host behavior\\n\\n## Dependencies\\n\\n- [references/host-capabilities.md](../../references/host-capabilities.md)\\n- [references/orchestration-contract.md](../../references/orchestration-contract.md)\\n- [references/workflow-ownership.md](../../references/workflow-ownership.md)\\n- [skills/create-verification-skill/SKILL.md](../../skills/create-verification-skill/SKILL.md)\\n\\n## Host limits\\n\\n- Missing independent workers permits serial source review only as reduced capability.\\n- Missing execution or app driving leaves live verification not-run and coverage blocked.\\n- Missing writes permits recommendations only; shipping requires explicit authorization.\\n- The canonical inventory and local dependencies are structurally reconciled; registered cases still require actual per-host observations.\\n- Both hosts remain not-run; structural checks do not prove behavior.\\n\\n## Source proof and release evidence\\n\\nCurrent host statuses and release evidence: [current parity inventory](../parity/inventory.json), [chatgpt host report](../testing/chatgpt.md), and [codex host report](../testing/codex.md). These records can advance after the source freeze. Structural catalog checks do not establish host support.\\n\\n### `skills/maintain-verification-skill/SKILL.md`\\n\\nDestination: [skills/maintain-verification-skill/SKILL.md](../../skills/maintain-verification-skill/SKILL.md).\\n\\nUpstream commit: `df581122cde17e6e27686b5a448bde23e4ad4318`. SHA-256: `515c0eaa054b3f6be1b1fb06f2c2f173c80fddb58bbcac57576f89c479bc68e8`.\\n\\nDisposition: `runtime`. Aggregate adaptation status: `adapted-reviewed`.\\n# Choose a workflow\\n\\nRead the [51-skill catalog](../skills/README.md) for complete triggers, inputs, outputs, dependencies and cases. The [24 principles](../principles/README.md) support decisions inside workflows. A workflow summary does not replace reading its full skill and required contracts.\\n\\n| Request | Start with | Expected artifact |\\n| --- | --- | --- |\\n| Understand runtime flow or historical reasons | [how](../skills/how.md), [why](../skills/why.md), [teach](../skills/teach.md) | Grounded explanation with source gaps |\\n| Compare competing shapes for one artifact | [Arena walkthrough](arena.md), [architect](../skills/architect.md) | Synthesized base, graft rationale and verification |\\n| Cover independent slices or race declared alternatives | [Swarm walkthrough](swarm.md) | Consolidated report with explicit gaps |\\n| Apply deliberate routing and proof discipline | [Poteto mode walkthrough](poteto-mode.md) | Selected playbook, checkpoint and inspected evidence |\\n| Review consequences or challenge a change | [blast-radius](../skills/blast-radius.md), [interrogate](../skills/interrogate.md) | Attributed risks and evidence |\\n| Resume, audit or record a run | [recall](../skills/recall.md), [show-me-your-work](../skills/show-me-your-work.md) | Scoped context or decision trail |\\n| Ask how to use pstack | [poteto-help](../skills/poteto-help.md) | One usable prompt and source |\\n| Review a source update | [upstream update walkthrough](../maintenance/upstream-updates.md) | Reviewed bundle, separate apply and acceptance gates |\\n\\nChoose one [controller](../../references/workflow-ownership.md). Retain the active controller on unqualified requests. With none active, keep host defaults and do not silently activate a mode. A helper advises the owner without taking ownership. Resolve an explicit request for both controllers before dispatch or edits.\\n\\nCheck [compatibility](../compatibility.md) and [capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md) before execution. Workers need observed spawn and inspected results. Sequential role use is reduced capability. Exact requested models need supported exposed selections; actual models remain unknown until observed. Source-only help stays bounded by authorized reads.\\n\\nPersonalization begins only after all baseline dispositions are reviewed and both hosts have actual supported-behavior evidence or explicit blocks. [automate-me](../skills/automate-me.md), [reflect](../skills/reflect.md) and [setup-pstack](../skills/setup-pstack.md) retain their own review and persistence gates. No workflow grants setup, external writes, integration or scheduling permission.\\n---\\nname: unslop\\ndescription: Cut AI tells from any writing. Must always apply.\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Unslop\\n\\nEdit text to remove AI patterns.\\n\\n## Process\\n\\n1. Scan for the patterns below.\\n2. Rewrite. Preserve meaning, match intended tone.\\n\\n## Patterns to detect and fix\\n\\nRule numbers are stable ids that other skills cite. A removed rule leaves a gap.\\n\\n### Content\\n\\n3. **Superficial -ing phrases.** \\\"highlighting...\\\", \\\"ensuring...\\\", \\\"reflecting...\\\", \\\"showcasing...\\\", \\\"fostering...\\\". Delete or expand with real sources.\\n5. **Vague attributions.** \\\"Experts believe\\\", \\\"Industry reports suggest\\\", \\\"Some critics argue\\\". Name the source or delete.\\n\\n### Language\\n\\n7. **AI vocabulary.** Additionally, crucial, delve, enduring, enhance, fostering, garner, interplay, intricate, landscape (abstract), pivotal, showcase, tapestry (abstract), testament, underscore, vibrant. Replace with plain words.\\n8. **Fancy ways to say \\\"is\\\".** \\\"serves as\\\", \\\"stands as\\\", \\\"boasts\\\", \\\"features\\\". Just say \\\"is\\\" or \\\"has\\\".\\n9. **\\\"Not just X, but Y.\\\"** State the point directly instead.\\n10. **Rule of three.** Forcing ideas into groups of three. Use the natural number.\\n11. **Synonym cycling.** Protagonist, main character, central figure, hero all in one paragraph. Pick one, repeat it.\\n12. **False ranges.** \\\"from X to Y\\\" where X and Y aren't on a meaningful scale. List topics directly.\\n\\n### Style\\n\\n13. **Em dash overuse.** Avoid em dashes entirely. Use periods or commas only (no parentheses, no en dashes, no hyphen-as-dash substitutes). If a thought needs separation, end the sentence or use a comma.\\n14. **Colon overuse.** Colons are fine before a list or example. Not as mid-sentence connectors. \\\"If you're coming from traditional automation: instead of registering event handlers, you describe conditions\\\" adds nothing with the colon. Rewrite to let the point stand on its own without comparison framing. \\\"Describing when the scheduler should fire works best as plain English.\\\" Same meaning, no crutch punctuation.\\n15. **Boldface overuse.** Don't bold every proper noun or acronym.\\n16. **Inline-header lists.** The tell is a bold label and colon that restates the line: \\\"**Performance:** Performance improved...\\\". Convert those to prose. A bold lead-in that ends in a period, names the item, and is followed by genuinely new detail (\\\"**Schema in TypeScript.** Tables live in one file.\\\") is fine, not a tell.\\n17. **Title case headings.** Use sentence case.\\n18. **Decorative emojis.** Remove from headings and bullets.\\n19. **Curly quotes.** Replace with straight quotes.\\n\\n### Communication artifacts\\n\\n20. **Chatbot phrases.** \\\"I hope this helps!\\\", \\\"Let me know if...\\\", \\\"Of course!\\\", \\\"Certainly!\\\", \\\"Found the smoking gun!\\\" Remove.\\n22. **Sycophantic tone.** \\\"Great question! You're absolutely right!\\\" Respond directly.\\n\\n### Filler\\n\\n23. **Filler phrases.** \\\"In order to\\\" becomes \\\"To\\\". \\\"Due to the fact that\\\" becomes \\\"Because\\\". \\\"It is important to note that\\\" gets deleted.\\n24. **Excessive hedging.** \\\"could potentially possibly be argued that it might\\\" becomes \\\"may\\\".\\n25. **Generic conclusions.** \\\"The future looks bright.\\\" State specific plans or facts.\\n\\n### Jargon\\n\\n26. **Abstract metaphor nouns.** Substrate, wedge, vector, locus, vantage, nexus, primitive (as noun), harness (as metaphor), surface (as in \\\"API surface\\\"), bedrock, scaffolding (as metaphor), modality, paradigm, gold-plating, ratchet (as metaphor), evacuate (for moving code), endgame, north star, flywheel. These read as technical but usually have a plainer concrete word. \\\"Substrate\\\" becomes \\\"base\\\". \\\"Wedge in\\\" becomes \\\"add\\\". \\\"Vector\\\" becomes \\\"way\\\" or \\\"method\\\". \\\"Gold-plating\\\" becomes \\\"more than the job needs\\\". \\\"Ratchet\\\" becomes the mechanism's real name or \\\"a limit that only tightens\\\". \\\"Evacuate\\\" becomes \\\"move out\\\". \\\"Endgame\\\" becomes \\\"the last phase\\\". Pick the concrete word.\\n\\n### Plain speech\\n\\n27. **Say what it does, not how it feels.** \\\"the database stays close at hand\\\", \\\"SQL you can read\\\", \\\"types that follow your schema\\\" name a feeling. The fix names the mechanism or a number: \\\"`.toSQL()` returns the exact string sent to the database\\\", \\\"a column rename fails the build\\\". Ask what the sentence tells the reader to do or know, then write that. If you can't restate it as a concrete instruction, fact, or number, cut it. One more check: if the sentence could appear unchanged in another project's docs, it says nothing about this one. Cut it.\\n28. **Shorten or split dense sentences.** If the reader has to backtrack to parse a sentence, break it in two or drop clauses. One idea per sentence.\\n29. **Active voice.** Prefer it. Catch \\\"is/are/was/were + past participle\\\" and name the actor: \\\"queries are validated\\\" becomes \\\"the compiler validates queries\\\", \\\"the file is parsed by the loader\\\" becomes \\\"the loader parses the file\\\". Passive is fine only when the actor is unknown or genuinely doesn't matter.\\n30. **Cut adverbs, or use a stronger verb.** \\\"runs quickly\\\" becomes \\\"is fast\\\" or the number. \\\"significantly improves\\\" becomes the measured delta. An adverb propping up a weak verb means the verb is wrong.\\n31. **Prefer the plain word.** \\\"utilize\\\" becomes \\\"use\\\", \\\"leverage\\\" becomes \\\"use\\\", \\\"facilitate\\\" becomes \\\"help\\\", \\\"numerous\\\" becomes \\\"many\\\", \\\"in the event that\\\" becomes \\\"if\\\". The fancier synonym is rarely clearer.\\n32. **Mannered prose.** Metaphor or flourish where a literal phrase exists: aphorisms (\\\"wire it or delete it\\\"), rhetorical fragments for effect, personified code (\\\"the plan holds it\\\"), figurative verbs (\\\"rides along\\\", \\\"stands on\\\"), stock framing phrases. \\\"A dial worth turning\\\" becomes \\\"a parameter worth varying\\\". Say what you mean. Rule 26 covers the metaphor nouns.\\n33. **Over-compression.** Dropped articles, verbless fragments, symbol-speak, and abbreviations that make the reader decode instead of read. \\\"Parser rejects bad date → exit 2, no write\\\" becomes \\\"The parser rejects a bad date, exits with code 2, and writes nothing.\\\" Write whole sentences with their articles and verbs, and spell out arrows and abbreviations.\\n\"}}\n{\"tool\":\"exec_command\",\"args\":{\"cmd\":\"cat /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/docs/principles/README.md; cat /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/references/model-roles.md; cat /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/principle-prove-it-works/SKILL.md; cat /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/create-verification-skill/SKILL.md\",\"max_output_tokens\":11000},\"return\":{\"chunk_id\":\"ceb0ad\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000006333,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":4343,\"output\":\"# Principle catalog\\n\\nGenerated from 51 reviewed leaf records, including 24 principles. handling of variants. |\\n# Model selection for portable roles\\n\\nUse [session capabilities](host-capabilities.md) before assigning a role. These [poteto](agent-prompts/poteto-agent.md) and [comment reviewer](agent-prompts/comment-sicko.md) prompts are instructions supplied to an exposed worker operation. Reading them does not register a custom agent or authorize delegation.\\n\\n| Role | Default requested model | Observed model | Supported effort | Fallback disclosure |\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n| poteto-agent | inherit-parent | unknown until observed | inherit-parent unless a supported explicit effort is verified | Record rejected selection, replacement request, observed result and limits. |\\n| comment-sicko | inherit-parent | unknown until observed | inherit-parent unless a supported explicit effort is verified | Record rejected selection, replacement request, observed result and limits. |\\n\\nFor each assignment, record requested model and effort, observed model and effort, supported effort values and their source, worker identity, and fallback disclosure. Use `unknown` for information the operation does not expose. `inherit-parent` records a selection policy, not an observed model name. Do not silently convert a requested model into an actual model.\\n\\nSelect an explicit model only from current exposed tool documentation or observed enumeration results. If enumeration is missing, disclose that limit and retain `inherit-parent`. Documentation examples are not a list of available models. Use only an effort supported for the selected model by the current operation; do not carry an incompatible inherited effort into an explicit selection.\\n\\nIf a selected model or effort is rejected, retain the rejection. Use an authorized inherited or supported alternative only after disclosing the changed selection. If the task requires that exact selection, stop and record blocked. A fallback never proves the original selection succeeded.\\n\\nIdentical observed models mean no model diversity. Different roles can supply distinct instructions on the same model. Unknown observed models leave diversity unverified, even when requested model names differ. Different observed models support a model-diversity statement only for those observed assignments. Worker independence still requires successful spawn and result inspection.\\n\\nWhen workers are unavailable, apply an authorized role sequentially and label reduced capability. Preserve scope and permission boundaries. Do not claim independent review, background execution, or full worker support from sequential role switching.\\n\\nThe cached documentation from https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/agent-configuration/subagents describes inheritance and optional local agent configuration. It is context only. This package writes no local custom-agent configuration and claims no current host model or worker support from that documentation.\\n---\\nname: principle-prove-it-works\\ndescription: \\\"Apply after completing a task, before declaring done. Verify against the real artifact (run the feature, read the actual value, inspect the diff), not a proxy, self-report, or 'it compiles.'\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Prove It Works\\n\\nVerify every task output by checking the real thing directly. Do not infer from proxies, self-reports, or \\\"it compiles.\\\"\\n\\n**Why:** Unverified work has unknown correctness. Indirect verification (file mtimes, output freshness, agent self-reports, cached screenshots) feels cheaper than direct observation. Acting on a wrong inference costs far more than checking the source.\\n\\nCheck the real thing, not a proxy:\\n- Check process liveness directly, not indirectly through derived state\\n- Read the actual value, not a cached or derived representation\\n- When verification fails, suspect the observation method before suspecting the system\\n\\n## Script the check when you can\\n\\nThe strongest proof is a deterministic script that re-runs the same comparison, not a one-time eyeball. Write the script, run it, and keep its output as an artifact a reviewer can re-run instead of trusting your word.\\n\\nKeep the artifact visible for the human. Commit it only for large or complex work where the trail has to be auditable later, like a big port or migration (the **show-me-your-work** skill).\\n\\n## Session capabilities and permissions\\n\\nUse the exposed tools and observed operations described in [host capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md). Run checks and retain artifacts only within the user's authorized scope and host permissions. If direct inspection or execution is unavailable or denied, state which outputs remain unverified and withhold completion for those outputs. A proposed script is not evidence that it ran.\\n---\\nname: create-verification-skill\\ndescription: \\\"Generate a project-local verification skill that drives your app the way a user does, any language, framework, or platform. Use for /create-verification-skill, \\\\\\\"make a control skill for this repo\\\\\\\", or when a project has no scripted way to prove UI/CLI/service behavior.\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Create a verification skill\\n\\nEvery serious project needs a scripted way to drive the real app and prove behavior: launch it, exercise a feature the way a user would, and capture evidence. This skill generates that as a project-local skill (`<project-local-skills>/verify-<app>/`) tailored to the repo. You write the generator's output for the next agent, not for a human: it will be read cold, mid-task, by an agent that has never seen the app.\\n\\n## Host binding and authoring boundaries\\n\\nRead [host capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md) and [workflow ownership](../../references/workflow-ownership.md). Discover the current host's project-local skill directory and registration requirements from exposed documentation and repository conventions before writing. In this document, `<project-local-skills>` means that observed directory, never a literal output path. If discovery cannot be confirmed, produce an authorized portable draft and mark registration unverified. Preserve the name, description and explicit invocation aliases; invocation metadata does not prove host discovery.\\n\\nBind repository reads, authorized local writes, execution and app-driving to exposed tools and observed results. Use an existing harness where available. Missing browser, PTY or execution capabilities remain explicit; documented recipes are not successful operations. Do not install dependencies, change credentials or settings, start listeners, or perform external writes without applicable user authorization. Follow the active owner's scope. When writes are denied, report the blocker without writing.\\n\\nIf app controls can be inspected but proof cannot run, author a draft tied to those actual controls, mark verification `not-run`, and list missing capabilities and unproved paths. Never fill unknown commands or selectors with invented values. A draft is not a verified deliverable.\\n\\n## 1. Interview the repo, not the user\\n\\nAnswer these from the codebase and only ask the user what you cannot observe:\\n\\n- **Surface:** what does a user actually touch? A web UI, a CLI/TUI, a desktop app, an API, a mobile app, a library? A repo can have several; pick the primary one and note the rest.\\n- **Run:** how does the app start locally? Prefer the repo's own documented dev command (package scripts, Makefile, README quickstart). Note ports, env vars, seed data, auth.\\n- **Drive:** how can an agent interact with it programmatically? Existing harnesses first, Playwright/Cypress specs, expect scripts, PTY helpers, curl-able endpoints, a debug port. Only then pick a generic recipe: browser/CDP for web and Electron, a tmux/PTY harness for CLI/TUI, plain HTTP for services.\\n- **Observe:** what evidence can be captured? Screenshots, terminal transcripts, response bodies, logs, exit codes, DB state.\\n- **Isolate:** can two instances run side by side (ports, data dirs, profiles)? If not, say so in the generated skill: refusing to double-drive a shared instance beats corrupting the user's session.\\n\\nIf the checkout doesn't build or start as-is, fix that first (or report it precisely) before generating; a skill written against a broken base teaches wrong steps. When an irrelevant missing asset blocks startup (a static dir the API never serves, a sample config), the generated skill may create it, clearly marked as verification scaffolding, and remove it in cleanup.\\n\\n## 2. Generate the skill\\n\\nWrite `<project-local-skills>/verify-<app>/SKILL.md` with YAML frontmatter (`name: verify-<app>` and a `description` that names the app, the surface, and when to reach for it, without frontmatter the skill never registers) and these sections, each grounded in what the interview actually found (no placeholders left):\\n\\n- **Launch:** the exact command that starts the app for verification, and how to tell it's ready (a log line, a port answering, a prompt). Include teardown. For a short-lived CLI or TUI there is no server to keep alive: launch means build the binary (or install deps) once, then start each drive in its own isolated PTY or tmux session.\\n- **Doctor:** one read-only check that answers \\\"is this instance worth driving?\\\", process up, right version/build, port owned by us, auth valid. An agent runs this first whenever anything looks off.\\n- **Drive:** the harness recipe with real selectors/commands from this repo, not examples. Prefer stable handles (ARIA labels, data attributes, prompt strings, route paths) over coordinates and tab order.\\n- **Evidence:** what to capture for a proof and where it goes. State the proof standards: exercise the real user path, not internal setters or test-only endpoints; capture the action and the resulting state, not just the final screen; verify side effects (files written, rows inserted, messages sent) alongside what's visible; mocks only where a production boundary already isolates the external system. When the safe path is a dry-run or test mode, verify what it actually skips by observing (files, network, git refs) rather than trusting its name: some dry-runs still touch the network or open a browser.\\n- **Cleanup:** how to tear down instances the run created. Never kill by process name; kill what you started. Cleanup removes instances and scratch state, never the evidence: proof artifacts survive the teardown, in a location the skill names.\\n- **Helpers:** any script the skill ships is executable and its invocation is shown in the skill body. A helper the reader has to reverse-engineer is not a helper.\\n\\n## 3. Seed the feature map\\n\\nCreate `<project-local-skills>/verify-<app>/features/README.md` plus one file per user-facing feature you can identify (aim for the top 3-5 to start, from routes, commands, menus, or docs). The Notes files are illustrative contracts, not executable instructions for this repo. Preserve their example commands when consulting them, but derive generated commands from the actual app. Follow the shape in [`references/feature-map-example/`](references/feature-map-example/), with a README index and one file per feature. Each file answers, from the user's point of view: what the feature is, how to reach it, how to drive it with the harness, and what observable end state proves it works. The four H2s are `Sub-features`, `How to get to it (user POV)`, `Driving it with <harness>`, and `Gotchas`. The map is the repo's maintained verification source; a proof that drives one convenient entry point is incomplete when the map lists others.\\n\\n## 4. Prove the generated skill before handing it over\\n\\nRun its own instructions end to end once: launch, doctor, drive ONE mapped feature (one is enough; the map exists so later runs can cover the rest), capture evidence, clean up. After cleanup, confirm the evidence still exists at the named location, a cleanup that eats the proof fails this step. Fix what fails, and run the generated cleanup after every failed iteration too, so broken attempts don't strand processes and ports. A generated skill that was never executed is a draft, not a deliverable.\\n\\n## 5. Offer the maintenance loop\\n\\nPoint the user at `/maintain-verification-skill` for keeping the map honest as the app changes. Suggest a cadence only if they ask.\\n\"}}\n{\"tool\":\"collaboration.list_agents\",\"args\":{\"path_prefix\":\"/root/stale_feature_map\"},\"return\":{\"agents\":[{\"agent_name\":\"/root/stale_feature_map\",\"agent_status\":\"running\"}]}}\n{\"tool\":\"exec_command\",\"args\":{\"cmd\":\"bun -e 'import {readFileSync,writeFileSync,appendFileSync} from \\\"node:fs\\\";import {createHash} from \\\"node:crypto\\\";const out=\\\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-013-20261016/fixtures/cases/stale-feature-map/outputs/\\\";writeFileSync(out+\\\"stale-feature-map.result.md\\\",\\\"Outcome: blocked at maintenance step 0.\\\\n\\\\nAssignment stale-feature-map-native.
